# SWE Subcategories — Build Plan

**Status: 2026-08-20 · 121 steps · 6 PRs · 2 repos · NOT YET IMPLEMENTED.**

## How to use this document

This is the **build plan** — the executable step list. The **decision record** (why each choice was made, the alternatives rejected, the measured evidence) is `docs/plans/2026-08-20-swe-subcategories.md`; this file does not repeat it.

Every `dependsOn` id in this document resolves to a step defined in this document. There are no external ids, no phantom ids, and no cycles. Work top-to-bottom within a PR; the order listed is already a valid topological order.

`⚠` marks an assertion or anchor that is load-bearing against a **silent** failure — a case where the wrong implementation returns 200/`ok`/`written: N` and persists nothing. Do not weaken a `⚠` test to make it pass.

### 1.2 The wire contract

**One field name: `subcategories` (plural) on a JOB; `subcategory` (singular) on a saved FILTER. Never mixed.**

**A. Live lane — existing `POST /api/internal/enrichment/results`, three new keys per item**

```
"subcategories":          list[str] | null      (default = _UNSET sentinel, NOT None)
"subcategory_confidence": float | null
"subcategory_source":     "rule"|"classify"|"backfill"|"judge"|"human"
```

**Four states, and they are four:**

| state | meaning | write |
|---|---|---|
| key **absent** | `_UNSET` | touch **neither** column — this is what makes the enricher knob a reversible deploy order instead of a code push. A model default of `None` would NULL every row on every ordinary tick. |
| `null` | never evaluated | `enrichment_subcategories = NULL`, source `NULL`. **This is the backfill queue.** |
| `[]` | evaluated, no specialty applies | `'{}'::text[]` + source. **TERMINAL** — leaves the queue, never re-queued. |
| `["backend"]` | labelled | ORDERED, index 0 primary, **MAX 2**. |

Validation mirrors `_valid()`'s soft-null contract (enrichment_writer.py:90-91): an out-of-enum slug is **dropped with a `warnings[]` entry**, never a 422, never a dropped batch; if every slug drops the value becomes `[]`, not null. >2 slugs → truncate + warn. `full_stack` present → drop `frontend`/`backend` + warn. Resolved category ≠ `software_engineering` with a non-empty list → force `NULL` + warn. `subcategory_confidence` MUST be null when the array is `[]` or null. `subcategory_source` MUST be present when the array is a list (including `[]`) and absent/null when it is null.

**B. Bulk drain (D2) — NEW `POST /api/internal/enrichment/subcategories`** (internal-key, same router)

```json
{"items":[{"source_id":"…","job_listing_id":"…","subcategories":["infrastructure_platform","backend"],
           "subcategory_confidence":0.82,"subcategory_source":"backfill","taxonomy_version":"v7+<12hex>"}]}
→ 200 {"written":N,"skipped":[…],"failed":[…],"warnings":[…]}
```

`SubcategoryResultItem` is **`extra='forbid'`** (one client, no legacy fleet — an unknown key must 422 loudly). Max 500 items. Same four states as (A) except the key may not be absent. Issues **only**:

```sql
UPDATE job_listings   SET enrichment_subcategories=%s, enrichment_subcategory_source=%s WHERE source_id=%s AND id=%s;
UPDATE job_enrichment SET subcategory_confidence=%s, taxonomy_version=%s WHERE source_id=%s AND job_listing_id=%s;
```

Nothing else — not the description, not category/level, not tags, not `enriched_at`.

**Human lock is PER-FIELD:** skip a row only when `enrichment_subcategory_source = 'human'`.

**C. Read side — JVN → SPA**

```
JobListingResponse.subcategories: list[str] | None   // NEVER coerce. `raw.subcategories ?? []` is forbidden.
JobFacetsResponse.subcategories:  list[FacetOption]  // FacetOption unchanged; the categories query already
                                                     // selects `NULL AS parent_slug` (enrichment_monitor.py:709)
PublicSettingsResponse: { "sweSubcategoriesEnabled": bool }   // GET /api/jobs/settings
```

`parentSlug` carries **two** meanings: on `job_levels` a FILTER-EXPANSION edge (`new_grad` in `entry`); on `job_subcategories` a GROUPING edge. It **must never** be fed into the level-expansion builder.

**D. The `[]`-collision, stated once so both sides test it**

- `job_listings.enrichment_subcategories = '{}'` → "evaluated, none apply", TERMINAL.
- `user_saved_filters.subcategory = '[]'` → "no filter selected, show EVERYTHING".

Same literal, opposite meanings, same Redux field name flows through both. Two tests, adjacent files, one comment naming it (`RA-7` + `SCHEMA-3`).

### 1.3 The one-of-each decisions

| Seam | Decision |
|---|---|
| **`app_settings`** | ONE table, ONE revision (**SCHEMA-2**). `key TEXT PK, value JSONB NOT NULL, updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_by TEXT NULL`. **NO seed row — absent means the code default**, so a fresh DB, a deleted flag and a rolled-back migration behave identically and the reader can never 500. One allowlisted key: `swe_subcategories_enabled` (bool, default `false`). ORM class `AppSetting` in `db_models.py` (JSONB already imported at **:37** — verified). **`'app_settings'` MUST be added to the `==` frozen set in `test_db_models.py`** (verified: function at :11, literal :13-37, exactly 23 names) or the whole backend suite fails. |
| **Flag endpoint** | **`GET /api/jobs/settings`**, unauthenticated, declared in `routers/jobs.py` between `/facets` (**:277**) and `/{source_id}/{job_id}` (**:292**). No `vercel.json` change: **:26** already rewrites `/api/jobs/:path(.*)`. Hook `useGetPublicSettingsQuery`, `keepUnusedDataFor: 60`, **fail-CLOSED**. It must NOT ride `/api/jobs/facets` — verified `getFacets` has no `providesTags` and `keepUnusedDataFor: 3600`, so a flag there is invisible for up to an hour and cannot be tag-invalidated. The backend does **not** gate `?subcategory=` on the flag; it is a UI reveal switch only. Consumer gate: `flag && (facets?.subcategories?.length ?? 0) > 0`. |
| **Proxy allowlist** | `api/jobs.ts` **lives at the repo root**. Verified on #252: `const ALLOWED_SUB_PATHS = new Set(['facets','search'])` at **:25**, used at **:45**; `REPEATABLE_PARAMS` at **:37**, loop at **:73**. Final membership: **`['facets','search','settings']`** — keep #252's constant NAME, add exactly one member. Dropping `'search'` 404s #252's whole endpoint. `jobs.serverless.test.ts` **already exists on #252** (verified via `git ls-tree`) — **EXTEND, never create.** `ADM-3` is sole owner of both; `RA-6` adds `'subcategory'` to `REPEATABLE_PARAMS` (a different line) and depends on `ADM-3` so the two edits serialize. |
| **Rubric file** | **`.claude/skills/taxonomy/SUBCATEGORIES.md`** (confirmed absent today). Accessor **`taxonomy.read_subcategory_rubric()`**, raising `FileNotFoundError`. Created by `ENR-TAX-2`. SKILL.md §1b stays **compact** (3,006 chars measured); the ten tie-breakers and long-form few-shots move out. Two callers only: `ENR-PB-1`'s `build_subcategory_prompt` and `EV-7`'s pre-labeller. **Provenance:** `taxonomy_version()` = `declared_version() + '+' + sha256(skill_text + '\x00' + rubric_text)[:12]` — without this, editing the rubric would not move the version hash, which is exactly the hole the split would otherwise open. |
| **v3→v4 integrator** | **ONE: `ENR-ST-1`.** It alone edits `SCHEMA_VERSION = 3 → 4` (verified store.py:**47**), writes `_migrate_v3_to_v4`, and wires `if version < 4 and has_inbox:` into the ladder after the v2→v3 line at **:229-230**, before `conn.executescript(_SCHEMA_SQL)` at **:231**. BF and P0 contribute column lists as text. **ONE scope table:** `backfill_scope`; `backfill_roster` is deleted. **ONE rehearsal: `P0-7`.** |
| **`subcategory_source`** | **Five values.** `rule` = deterministic title/tag regex, no model call. `classify` = live Tier-1 tick. `backfill` = Tier-2. `judge` = judge overwrote. `human` = admin correction, and the value the D2 push treats as a lock. Landed as `SUBCATEGORY_SOURCES: frozenset` in JVN `enrichment_writer.py` (SCHEMA-3a) and ENR `taxonomy.py` (ENR-TAX-1); asserted cross-repo by `SCHEMA-15` + `ENR-PAR-2`. The eval seed's separate provenance column is renamed **`gold_subcategory_provenance`** (`human \| human_audited \| prelabel_agreed \| contract`) so two similarly-named columns with disjoint enums cannot be confused. |
| **`full_stack` expansion** | `{frontend: ['frontend','full_stack'], backend: ['backend','full_stack']}`, keyed on the SELECTED slug (same direction as `_LEVEL_FILTER_EXPANSION`, database.py:86). One-way: selecting Full Stack stays exact. **SERVER is the sole expander for `/api/jobs/search`** (`RA-4`). **CLIENT is the sole expander for the client-side path** (`FE-ST-4`) — post-#252 that is Companies/Graph + demo only. Double expansion would persist `['backend','full_stack']` into saved filters and chips. **One committed oracle fixture read by both languages:** `src/backend/api/tests/fixtures/subcategory_filter_oracle.json`, authored by `RA-4`, read by the pytest half (`RA-4`) and the vitest half (`FE-ST-4`) and by `FE-CT-2`'s expansion-map assertion. |

### 1.4 The alembic chain (Order A — pinned)

Verified by reading the files on `origin/feat/jobs-search-endpoint`.

| revision | down_revision | owner | phase |
|---|---|---|---|
| `1d2d6c17acfc` retire poke board | `d8b52c04f6e3` | existing — **main's head** | — |
| `4b5d40dbc774` open+category keyset index | `1d2d6c17acfc` | **PR #252** | — |
| `536c1cddcd28` job_tags trigram index | `4b5d40dbc774` | **PR #252 — its TRUE head** | — |
| `<rev_A>` `add_job_subcategories_structure` | **`'536c1cddcd28'`** | **SCHEMA-1** | 1 |
| `<rev_B>` `add_app_settings` | `<rev_A>` | **SCHEMA-2** | 1 |
| `<rev_C>` `retire_project_manager_category` | `<rev_B>` | **SCHEMA-11** | 1 |
| `<rev_D>` `seed_job_subcategories` | `<rev_C>` | **SCHEMA-7** | 2 |
| `<rev_E>` `add_subcategory_to_user_saved_filters` | `<rev_D>` | **SCHEMA-8** | 2 |
| `<rev_F>` `add_subcategory_coverage_index` | `<rev_E>` | **SCHEMA-12** — CONDITIONAL on RA-8 | 2 |

**Every revision in this epic is authored by the SCHEMA workstream. No other workstream emits one.** Any draft pinning `4b5d40dbc774` is **off by one**.

**Two heads is a boot-time OUTAGE**, not a warning: `api/migrations.py:100` runs `command.upgrade(cfg, "head")` — singular — inside the FastAPI lifespan and re-raises (verified). `test_alembic_single_head.py:67-79` says exactly that and forbids `alembic merge`.

### 1.5 Order-B fallback (if #252 stalls) — the corrected procedure

Real graph: `d8b52c04f6e3 → 1d2d6c17acfc → 4b5d40dbc774 → 536c1cddcd28`, where the last two exist **only on #252**.

1. `<rev_A>.down_revision = '1d2d6c17acfc'` (main's head). Our chain runs to head **H** — whatever `alembic heads` prints on the trunk when #252 rebases: SCHEMA-8's revision, or SCHEMA-12's if it shipped. **Do not hardcode H.**
2. **ONE compensating edit, on the `feat/jobs-search-endpoint` branch, before it merges: `4b5d40dbc774.down_revision = H`.** Leave `536c1cddcd28.down_revision = '4b5d40dbc774'` untouched.
3. Result: `1d2d6c17acfc → <rev_A> → … → H → 4b5d40dbc774 → 536c1cddcd28`. One head.

**Why contract decision 4 is wrong:** editing `536c1cddcd28.down_revision` instead leaves `4b5d40dbc774` parented on `1d2d6c17acfc` with **no child** — so `4b5d40dbc774` *and* `536c1cddcd28` are both heads, `command.upgrade(cfg,'head')` raises `Multiple head revisions are present` in the lifespan, and the container never passes `railway.toml`'s healthcheck.

**The rule, stated generally: re-parent the OLDEST revision of the stalled branch, never its newest.** Verify with `alembic heads` printing exactly one revision and `pytest api/tests/test_alembic_single_head.py -q`.

Doing both edits re-forks the graph. Exactly one, either way.

## 3. The steps, by PR

Within each PR the order below is a valid topological order — no step precedes its dependency. `⚠` marks a step carrying a certified silent-failure assertion.

---

### PR-A — Rename only *(ships standalone, zero backend dependency)*

**`FE-CP-1` — "Job title" → "Job category" at all three sites + three false tooltips**

- *files:* `src/frontend/src/components/recent-jobs-page/RecentJobsFilters.tsx`, `components/companies-page/GraphFilters.tsx`, `components/saved-filters/CategoryLevelDefaults.tsx`
- *changes:* Pure copy, no imports, no control swap, no flag. `label="Job title"` → `"Job category"` at RecentJobsFilters (**:143 post-#252**), GraphFilters (**:89**), CategoryLevelDefaults (**:77**), plus its heading (:67) and save-button label (:98). Delete the FALSE clause "Jobs not yet enriched still appear." — **exactly three occurrences, all in CategoryLevelDefaults.tsx** (body :70-73, category tooltip :81, level tooltip :88); `useHydrateSavedFilters` pushes the saved category into a filter that hides unenriched rows, so all three lie.
- *dependsOn:* `[]`
- *tests:* Nothing breaks — verified on #252, `git grep 'Job title' -- src/frontend/src/__tests__` returns one prose comment (ListSection.test.tsx:89). Add ONE `getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Job category' })` assertion per filter-bar test file so the rename is pinned. Do **not** rename `label="Category"` in FacetMultiSelect/FacetSelect tests — arbitrary fixture props.
- *acceptance:* `git grep -n 'Job title\|job title' src/frontend/src` returns only legitimate posting-title references; `git grep 'not yet enriched still appear' src/frontend/src` returns nothing; `npm run type-check` clean; full suite green.
- *risk:* low · *effort:* S

**`FE-CL-1` — Changelog entry #1: the rename + the already-shipped department chip**

- *files:* `src/frontend/src/config/changelog.ts`
- *changes:* New entry at index 0 (line 21), id `job-category-rename-2026-08`. Also announces the removed department chip, which **shipped to `main` in PR #260 "Remove the ATS department chip from the job card" (`d6433b3`, merged 06:35 on 2026-08-20)** and is user-visible with no announcement. Do **not** edit `default-90d-and-job-title` (:93-104) — it is a dated public record; this entry reads as its correction.

**`ADM-4` ⚠ — `apply_correction`: subcategory tri-state, non-SWE forcing, and THE DATA-LOSS FIX**

- *files:* `services/enrichment_monitor.py`, `routers/admin.py`, `models.py`, `api/tests/test_enrichment_admin.py`
- *changes:* Anchors **re-verified** (the draft was off by 2-6 lines throughout): `apply_correction` at **:348**; validation **:370-377**; ⚠ **the UNCONDITIONAL `UPDATE job_listings SET enrichment_category=%s, enrichment_level=%s, enrichment_status='done', enrichment_claimed_at=NULL WHERE …` at :379-384**; the `human_corrected_at = now()` stamp at **:416**; read-back SELECT :425-433; returned dict :435-445. `apply_confirmation` :453 (projection :504-512); `request_reenrich` :535 (reset UPDATE :545-550); `_facet_slugs` :343. **THE FAILURE THIS CLOSES:** appending `enrichment_subcategories = %s` unconditionally means a level-only correction NULLs the array **and then locks the row**, so no backfill can ever repair it. The writer's demote edit **MOVES OUT** into SCHEMA-3, which owns `enrichment_writer.py` — the draft's file overlap was a merge conflict waiting to happen. `models.py`: `AdminEnrichmentCorrectionRequest` (**:1182**, `extra="forbid"` at **:1192-1194** — verified) and `AdminEnrichmentCorrectionResponse` (:1218) each gain `subcategories: list[str] | None = None`. **`extra='forbid'` is why this MUST precede ADM-8 — a UI sending the key against the old model 422s EVERY correction.** `admin.py` (:749-777) passes `subcategories=body.subcategories` **AND `subcategories_provided='subcategories' in body.model_fields_set`** — `None` and "key absent" are the same value at the handler and only `model_fields_set` separates them. **THE WRITE RULE, verbatim in the docstring:** SWE + not sent → **UNTOUCHED** (the fix); SWE + list → validated, primary first; SWE + null → NULL (explicit re-queue); non-SWE + not sent → forced `'{}'`; non-SWE + empty/null → forced `'{}'`; non-SWE + non-empty → **409**. Validation beside :370-377: strip+lower, drop empties, **DEDUPE PRESERVING ORDER (never `set()`)**, over-length → `CorrectionError`, unknown slug → `CorrectionError`, against `_facet_slugs(cur,'job_subcategories') or set(SUBCATEGORY_SLUGS)` — the live-dims-first pattern already two lines above. The UPDATE becomes a **dynamic SET list**.

- *tests:* ⚠ **THE CERTIFIED ROUND TRIP.** Extend `test_enrichment_admin.py::TestAdminEnrichmentCorrect` (**:135**); `_seed_flagged_job` (**:23**) gains a `subcategories=None` kwarg that UPDATEs the array.
  1. **`test_level_only_correction_leaves_subcategories_intact`** — seed a row with `enrichment_subcategories = '{backend,full_stack}'`; POST a correction body containing **only** `{"level":"senior"}` (no `subcategories` key); assert **(a)** 200, **(b)** `SELECT enrichment_subcategories` is *still* `{backend,full_stack}` (**not NULL, not `{}`**), **(c)** `enrichment_level = 'senior'`, **(d)** `human_corrected_at IS NOT NULL`. *Adding the field is not enough — this is the assertion.*
  2. `test_explicit_null_requeues` — body `{"subcategories": null}` → the column becomes NULL.
  3. `test_explicit_empty_is_terminal` — body `{"subcategories": []}` → `'{}'`.
  4. `test_subcategories_provided_is_false_for_absent_key` — unit-assert `'subcategories' not in body.model_fields_set` on a body that omits it, so nobody "simplifies" the `_UNSET` branch into `is None`.
  5. non-SWE + non-empty → 409; ordered dedupe `['backend','backend','frontend'] → ['backend','frontend']`; unknown slug → 409.
- *acceptance:* `pytest api/tests/test_enrichment_admin.py -k Correct -q` green against a clean DB. Manually: correct a row's level only and confirm `SELECT enrichment_subcategories` is unchanged in Postgres. · *risk:* **high** · *effort:* M

**`FE-CT-3` — `getFacets` tolerates and NORMALIZES a missing `subcategories` array**

- *files:* `features/jobs/jobsApi.ts`, `__tests__/features/jobs/jobsApi.facets.test.ts`
- *changes:* **POST-#252 ANCHORS** (the draft's 598-620 are pre-#252 and no longer exist). On `feat/jobs-search-endpoint` the endpoint is **jobsApi.ts:317-340**: doc comment 314-316, queryFn 318-338, the hard guard `if (!Array.isArray(facets?.categories) || !Array.isArray(facets?.levels))` at **:326**, `return { data: facets };` at **:329**, `keepUnusedDataFor: 3600` at **:339**, the destructured export block at 344-348. **Do NOT add `subcategories` to the hard guard at :326.** Instead replace :329 with `return { data: { ...facets, subcategories: Array.isArray(facets.subcategories) ? facets.subcategories : [] } };`. **Reason:** if the check were strict, a backend that has not shipped the catalog makes the WHOLE query error, `data` becomes undefined, and every consumer falls back to `FALLBACK_CATEGORIES` / `FALLBACK_LEVELS` — **regressing the LIVE category dropdown to the stale constant (project_manager included) and the level dropdown with it, on every Vercel-before-Railway deploy window.** Normalizing to `[]` means an old backend renders exactly today's flat dropdown with no chevron. Update the doc comment (314-316) to state the catalog is ADDITIVE and a missing `subcategories` is a SUPPORTED response, not an error. Leave `keepUnusedDataFor: 3600` alone and say why in the same comment.

- *files:* `services/enrichment_monitor.py`, `routers/admin.py`, `routers/internal_enrichment.py`, `models.py`, `api/tests/test_enrichment_admin.py`, `api/tests/test_internal_enrichment.py`

#### Stage 1

**`ENR-TAX-1` — `taxonomy.py`: `SUBCATEGORIES` frozenset + `validate_subcategories` (pure, no file reads)**

- *files:* `src/job_enricher/taxonomy.py`, `tests/test_taxonomy.py` (new)
- *changes:* **REVISED from the draft:** the §1b text-slicer is REMOVED from this step (it sliced `## 1b.` out of SKILL.md; under D3 the rubric is a separate file, so the accessor moves to `ENR-TAX-2` where the file exists). What remains is **pure Python with zero file I/O**, so `dependsOn: []` and it unblocks everything downstream without waiting on the SKILL.md edit. Add beside `CATEGORIES` (**taxonomy.py:28-37**) and `LEVELS` (**:38-40**), under the same "Contract of Record" comment at :23-27: `SUBCATEGORIES: frozenset[str]` with EXACTLY the 15 slugs (verified alphabetical; also label-alphabetical); `SUBCATEGORY_PARENT = 'software_engineering'`; `MAX_SUBCATEGORIES = 2`; `_FULL_STACK_SUPPRESSES = frozenset({'frontend','backend'})`; ⚠ **`SUBCATEGORY_SOURCES = frozenset({'rule','classify','backfill','judge','human'})` — WIDENED from the draft's three per §1.3.** Add `validate_subcategories(category, value) -> tuple[list[str]|None, list[tuple[str,str]], list[str]]` returning `(subcategories, bad, warnings)`. ⚠ **Do NOT change `validate_facets`' arity — verified unpacked positionally at `cli.py:440` (`category, level, bad = tax.validate_facets(...)`) and `cli.py:1038`, and imported by `tests/test_scoring.py`.** Rules, **in code because a prompt is not a rail**: (a) `value is None` → `(None, [], [])` — NULL means "never evaluated" and IS the backfill queue; (b) a bare `str` is tolerantly wrapped to a 1-list, any other non-list (dict/int/bool) → `(None, [('subcategories', repr(value))], [])`, **never raises**; (c) non-string elements → `bad`; (d) elements outside `SUBCATEGORIES` → `bad`; (e) dedup preserving first-seen order; (f) if `full_stack` survives, drop `frontend`/`backend` and append a **WARNING — not `bad`; it is a rule application, not a hallucination**; (g) truncate to `MAX_SUBCATEGORIES` with a warning — **truncation is never `bad` and must never set `needs_human`**; (h) `category != SUBCATEGORY_PARENT` (including `category is None`) with a non-empty list → `(None, [('subcategories', value)], [])` — **the leak rule, which is what makes `subcategory_leak_rate` structurally zero in prod while still measurable on raw model output in eval**; (i) an explicit `[]` on an SWE row → `([], [], [])`, a legitimate answer. **Do NOT touch `content_hash()` here** — ENR-TAX-2 changes provenance, and splitting that change away from the file it covers would create a window where the hash lies.
- *dependsOn:* `[]`
- *tests:* NEW `tests/test_taxonomy.py` (**no such file exists today — verified against the 15 files in tests/**): `len(SUBCATEGORIES)==15`; `sorted(SUBCATEGORIES)` equals the literal alphabetical list (freezes the enum against a typo); disjoint from `CATEGORIES` and `LEVELS`; `SUBCATEGORY_SOURCES` is exactly the five. `validate_subcategories`: `['backend','frontend']` passes; ⚠ **unknown slug `'ai_ml'` lands in `bad` — the correct unknown-slug fixture, since `ai_ml` was killed in r2 when AI split into `ai_engineering`/`ml_engineering`, so it is a real dead slug**; non-SWE parent with `['backend']` → `(None, bad non-empty)`; `category=None` with a list → `(None, bad non-empty)`; bare `'backend'` → `['backend']`; **dict/int/True → `(None, bad)` and NEVER raises**; 3 slugs truncate to 2 with a warning and **EMPTY `bad`**; `['full_stack','frontend']` → `['full_stack']` + warning, empty `bad`; `['backend','backend','frontend']` dedups to `['backend','frontend']`; ⚠ **explicit `[]` on an SWE row stays `[]`; `None` stays `None` (the tri-state assertion).**
- *acceptance:* `uv run pytest tests/test_taxonomy.py -q` green, and `uv run python -c "from job_enricher import taxonomy as t; print(len(t.SUBCATEGORIES), t.validate_subcategories('business_ops',['backend']))"` prints `15 (None, [('subcategories', ['backend'])], [])`. `uv run pytest tests/ -q` is still green (no existing call site changed). · *risk:* low · *effort:* S

**`ENR-ST-1` — `store.py` v3→v4: THE single migration integrator** *(corrected — see §0 ruling 7)*

- *files:* `src/job_enricher/store.py`, `tests/test_store.py`
- *changes:* **RENAMED from `ST-1`. Anything that pointed at `ST-1` must point here.** Sole author of `SCHEMA_VERSION`, `_migrate_v3_to_v4` and the `_SCHEMA_SQL` DDL for this epic; BF-1, P0-4 and OPS contribute column lists as text and **none of them edits the migration.**

The distinction is load-bearing and must never be collapsed**); `subcategory_confidence REAL`; `subcategory_source TEXT` (⚠ **one of the FIVE `SUBCATEGORY_SOURCES` — corrected from the draft's three**; the only thing that makes a rollback scoped instead of nuclear, and the only way to tell the compact-rubric classify lane's labels from the full-rubric backfill lane's); `subcategory_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`; `pre_judge_subcategories TEXT` (mirrors the existing pre_judge_* trio at :110-112); `subcategory_run_id INTEGER` (**NULL = not written by a backfill run; scopes rollback to ONE run**); `subcategory_sent_at TEXT` (**NULL = this row's subcategory has not shipped to JVN; this is what D2's throttled drain walks, and it is why the bulk `sent_at` re-open stays refused**).
  3. ⚠ **THREE new `ticks` columns** in the block at **:125-141** — `swe_classified`, **`swe_subcat_evaluated`**, `swe_subcategorized`, all `INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` — **AND all three names added to `_TICK_COUNTER_COLS` (:166-171).** Not optional decoration: **`bump_tick_counters` filters `counters.items()` against that frozenset at :632-634 and SILENTLY DROPS anything absent, so omitting one makes BF-11's drift breaker read 0 forever with no error anywhere.** *The draft carried only two; `swe_subcat_evaluated` is what `subcategory_empty_drift` is computed from.* The draft's `backfilled` and `classify_deferred` are **DELETED** — the backfill lane's counters live in `backfill_runs`.
  4. **TWO new tables, in `_SCHEMA_SQL` ONLY — NOT in `_migrate_v3_to_v4`.** Verified mechanism: `init_schema` runs the migration ladder (**:225-230**) and THEN `conn.executescript(_SCHEMA_SQL)` at **:231**, and every table there is `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, **so a new table is created identically on a fresh DB and on an upgraded one with zero migration code. Only new COLUMNS on existing tables need ALTERs.** ⚠ **`backfill_runs` uses BF-1's FULL column list verbatim** — `(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, uuid TEXT, started_at TEXT NOT NULL, ended_at TEXT, status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running', kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'label', engine TEXT, model TEXT, taxonomy_version TEXT, rubric_version TEXT, deadline_at TEXT, selected INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, rule_labelled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, tag_labelled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, model_labelled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, empty_labelled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, nulled_slugs INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, echo_mismatch INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, unanswered INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, transport_errors INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, parse_errors INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, errors INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, retired INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, model_calls INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, pushed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, push_batches INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, push_rejected INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, push_skipped_human INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, push_nulled_by_jvn INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, pool_pressure_events INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, aborted_reason TEXT, notes TEXT)` — **`kind` is 'label' or 'push': ONE ledger, two lanes, so the morning report reads one table**, and `rubric_version` is stamped separately from `taxonomy_version` because **without a second stamp a run cannot prove which rubric produced its labels.** *(The draft's truncated table omitted `kind`, `rubric_version` and all eleven push/echo counters.)* Plus `backfill_scope(source_id TEXT NOT NULL, job_listing_id TEXT NOT NULL, imported_at TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (source_id, job_listing_id))` — **`backfill_roster` is DELETED and must not appear anywhere.**
  5. ⚠ **BOTH indexes** beside `idx_enrichments_unsent` (**:122-123**): `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_enrichments_subcat_backlog ON enrichments(category, subcategory_attempts) WHERE subcategories IS NULL;` (the two-column form the selector's attempts predicate actually uses) **and** `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_enrichments_subcat_push ON enrichments(subcategory_sent_at) WHERE subcategories IS NOT NULL AND subcategory_sent_at IS NULL;` *(the second was dropped in the draft)*.
  6. `_migrate_v3_to_v4(conn)` immediately above `_migrate_v2_to_v3` (**:816**), modelled on it EXACTLY: read `PRAGMA table_info(enrichments)` and `PRAGMA table_info(ticks)` into name sets, `conn.execute('BEGIN')`, one guarded `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN` per missing column, `COMMIT`, `except: ROLLBACK; raise`. **ALTER-only — ten columns, no table rebuild, both primary keys unchanged — and idempotent, so a half-applied migration re-runs cleanly.** Defer the version stamp to `init_schema`'s final PRAGMA (**:232**) exactly as v3 does. Wire `if version < 4 and has_inbox: _migrate_v3_to_v4(conn)` into `init_schema` immediately after the v2→v3 line at **:229-230**.

  **Do NOT add store METHODS here:** `record_subcategories` is BF-8's, the selector is BF-4's, the backlog counts are BF-10's, and the `backfill_runs` accessors are BF-1's. **This step is DDL plus projection so it can be reviewed as one migration and rehearsed once.**

**`ENR-ENG-1` — The `response_schema` seam: an optional per-call schema, no behaviour change, four signatures plus the fake**

- *files:* `engines/base.py`, `engines/ollama.py`, `engines/claude_code.py`, `engines/opencode_cli.py`, `tests/test_engines.py`
- *changes:* **This is bigger than "one line to ollama.py" and it is worth naming why, all re-verified:** `_call(self, prompt: str) -> str` is declared abstract at **base.py:62** and implemented with that EXACT signature at **ollama.py:34**, **claude_code.py:29**, **opencode_cli.py:25** AND by the test fake `_ScriptedEngine._call(self, prompt)` at **tests/test_engines.py:231**. **There is NO per-call structured-output seam anywhere, and adding a keyword to only some implementations produces a `TypeError` the moment the base passes it.** All five change together, in one commit, with **ZERO behaviour change** — so this can ship and be reverted alone. `base.py`: `def _call(self, prompt: str, *, response_schema: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> str` (**:62**); `_timed(fn, prompt)` (**:131-134**) becomes `_timed(fn)` taking a zero-arg callable, called as `_timed(partial(self._call, prompt, response_schema=response_schema))` at **:108** and `_timed(partial(self._call, prompt + _RETRY_NUDGE, response_schema=response_schema))` at **:114** (`from functools import partial`). ⚠ **Threading through the retry is not optional: a repair attempt that drops the schema would return unconstrained output on exactly the calls that already went wrong once.** `_call_with_repair` (**:71-76**) gains the kwarg. `classify` (**:65-66**) and `judge` (**:68-69**) keep passing nothing, **so their emitted bytes are byte-identical to today.** Add a class attribute `subcategory_schema: dict[str, Any] | None = None` on `ChunkEngine` (:60 area, beside `name`). `ollama.py`: accept the kwarg and use `"format": response_schema or _ARRAY_SCHEMA` in the POST body (**:42**). `claude_code.py` and `opencode_cli.py`: accept and **IGNORE** the kwarg — neither `claude -p` nor `opencode run` has a structured-output flag; **one documented line each, so a future reader does not assume the constraint is enforced there** (the tolerant `extract_json_array` at **prompts.py:118-155** plus `validate_subcategories` are what protect those paths, which is exactly why the validator's rules are in code rather than in the prompt). `tests/test_engines.py:231`: update `_ScriptedEngine._call` to the new signature and **RECORD the schema it was handed** (append to a list) so ENR-ENG-3 and BF-6 can assert on it. **Deliberately NOT in this step:** `positional_repair_on_count_match` — that flag is BF-6's, and it is dangerous (a reordered batch writes row A's labels onto row B, the value is non-NULL so the row leaves the selector permanently, and the NULL tri-state cannot detect it). **Keeping it out of the seam step means the seam can ship on its own merits.**
- *dependsOn:* `[]`
- *tests:* ⚠ **the existing `test_ollama_sends_structured_format_and_temperature_zero` (:115-120) still passes UNCHANGED — that is the no-regression proof and the diff must not touch it**; a new test asserting ollama posts a supplied schema VERBATIM as `body['format']`; new tests that `ClaudeCodeEngine` and `OpenCodeEngine` accept `response_schema=` without raising **and produce the SAME argv as before (assert the subprocess arg list is byte-identical)**; `_ScriptedEngine` still drives the whole existing repair/retry suite (:248-430) with the new signature; ⚠ **a test that the retry attempt receives the same `response_schema` as the first attempt.**
- *acceptance:* `uv run pytest tests/test_engines.py -q` green **with ZERO changes to the existing ollama/claude/opencode argv and body assertions — the diff adds tests, it does not alter them.** `git diff --stat` shows five files and no change to `prompts.py`. · *risk:* low · *effort:* M

**`ENR-ENG-3` — `ollama._SUBCATEGORY_SCHEMA`: the enum-constrained schema, built from the frozenset, without a layering inversion**

- *files:* `engines/ollama.py`, `tests/test_engines.py`
- *changes:* **NARROWED HARD:** `build_subcategory_prompt`, `ChunkEngine.subcategory` + the backfill timeout + the `build_engine` timeout override **all move to `ENR-PB-1`** (they were previously shed to a step that never claimed them — see §0 ruling 1), and `config.py` leaves this step's file list entirely. What remains is the one thing neither BF step specifies: **the schema itself.** Add `_SUBCATEGORY_SCHEMA` beside `_ARRAY_SCHEMA` (**verified ollama.py:17**), ⚠ **BUILT FROM `sorted(tax.SUBCATEGORIES)` rather than a literal list so it can never drift** — an array of objects whose `subcategories` property is `{"type":"array","maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum": sorted(tax.SUBCATEGORIES)}}`, with `job_listing_id`/`source_id` as strings and `subcategory_confidence` as `{"type":["number","null"]}`. Requires one new import, `from job_enricher import taxonomy as tax` — **the same package-internal dependency `prompts.py` already has at :18.** Expose it as `OllamaEngine.subcategory_schema = _SUBCATEGORY_SCHEMA` (a class attribute overriding `ChunkEngine.subcategory_schema = None` from ENR-ENG-1). `ENR-PB-1`'s `subcategory()` then calls `self._call_with_repair(..., response_schema=self.subcategory_schema)` and gets the constrained schema on ollama and `None` on the two subprocess engines, **which ignore it anyway.** ⚠ **WHY NOT AN IMPORT (a deliberate deviation from contract decision 7's incidental wording, ACCEPTED in §0 ruling 12):** the draft had `base.py` import `_SUBCATEGORY_SCHEMA` from `ollama.py` so `subcategory()` could pass it. **That inverts the layering — the abstract base importing a concrete engine — and drags httpx into every import path that touches `ChunkEngine`.** The class-attribute override keeps the contract's SYMBOL NAME (`ollama._SUBCATEGORY_SCHEMA`), keeps the six-place byte parity intact, and keeps `base.py` dependency-free. **A response schema is an engine capability; the engine should own it.** Leave `_ARRAY_SCHEMA` permissive for classify/judge — **constraining every classify field is a far larger blast radius on the lane that is already at capacity**, and the classify path's protection is `validate_facets` + `validate_subcategories` in code.
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-TAX-1","ENR-ENG-1"]`
- *tests:* ⚠ **`_SUBCATEGORY_SCHEMA['items']['properties']['subcategories']['items']['enum'] == sorted(tax.SUBCATEGORIES)` exactly, as a list INCLUDING ORDER (the drift guard, and the reason it is built rather than typed)**; `maxItems == tax.MAX_SUBCATEGORIES`. ⚠ **THE TWO-SCHEMA TEST, both halves against a respx-mocked ollama:** a call made with `response_schema=OllamaEngine.subcategory_schema` posts a body whose `format` is the ENUM-CONSTRAINED schema with 15 alphabetical entries; **and in the SAME test file, `OllamaEngine.classify(rows)` still posts `format == {"type":"array","items":{"type":"object"}}` — proving the two lanes do not cross.** `ClaudeCodeEngine.subcategory_schema is None` and `OpenCodeEngine.subcategory_schema is None`.
- *acceptance:* `uv run pytest tests/test_engines.py -q` green; a respx-mocked subcategory call shows the 15 alphabetical slugs while the classify call in the neighbouring test still shows the permissive two-key array schema. · *risk:* low · *effort:* S

**`P0-3` — Close the capacity leak: count it, retire poison rows, and give retired rows a way back**

- *files:* `cli.py`, `store.py`, `enrich_config.py`, `health.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`, `tests/test_store.py`, `tests/test_enrich_config.py`, `tests/test_metrics.py`

Folding an order change into a migration is exactly how a declined decision ships anyway.** The starved-row COUNT is surfaced instead (P0-1's report + a `starved_null_recency` key on `collect_health`, absorbed from deleted OPS-1), and the remedy becomes a separate, explicitly-approved decision; `ORDER BY COALESCE(first_seen_at, claimed_at) DESC` is recorded in the runbook as the non-mutating candidate — **still an order change, still needs a yes.** **REMOVED — the `classify:engine_timeout_seconds` knob.** ⚠ **Its premise is verified FALSE:** `OllamaEngine.__init__` (**ollama.py:23-32**) takes `timeout: float = 300.0` as a **DEFAULT PARAMETER**, and `build_engine` already overrides it for every engine — **`timeout = settings_obj.engine_timeout_seconds` at engines/base.py:162**, passed at :167/:173/:179, defined at **config.py:40** with env `ENGINE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`. **ADDED:** the missing un-fail path — an `EngineError` bumps `errors` by the chunk size and increments `inbox.attempts` on every row in that chunk; at `max_attempts` the row flips to `'failed'`; a `cli unfail` command returns them.
- *dependsOn:* `["P0-1","P0-2"]`

- *acceptance:* After deploy plus three ticks: `cli health` shows `inbox.cleaned` **strictly decreasing tick-over-tick** and `failed_rows` visible in the printed report. · *risk:* medium · *effort:* M

- *changes:* **THIS IS THE D3 STEP. Three things land in ONE commit because splitting them opens a window where the recorded hash does not cover the rubric that produced the label.**
  ⚠ **EDIT 1, BEFORE ANYTHING ELSE: SKILL.md line 4, `version: v6` → `version: v7`.** Verified at **cli.py:1157-1180**: `_version_below` treats a same-declared-N file with a new hash as **NOT below** and only logs a warning, **so any version-keyed operation against a v6-declared-but-mutated file is a silent no-op.** This byte is also the one-way door for the eval baselines, which is why this step depends on `EV-2`/`EV-2b` rather than saying so in prose.
  **(A) NEW FILE `.claude/skills/taxonomy/SUBCATEGORIES.md` (~6,900 chars, ~59 lines).** Frontmatter mirroring SKILL.md's (`name: taxonomy-subcategories`, a description stating it is loaded ONLY by the subcategory-only lane, **`version: v7` kept in lockstep with SKILL.md**). Contents: **(1)** a preamble stating everything here is downstream of an already-made `software_engineering` verdict — **the arrow never runs backwards** — and that 0-2 slugs, most specific first, with `[]` legitimate; **(2)** the 15-row `| slug | one-line decision rule |` table with the FULL rules from §03 (ai_engineering: calls a model it did not train, the model is a dependency; backend: server-side code consumed by the product's own users or client apps; data_engineering: the deliverable is the movement and shaping of data; devops_sre: the mandate is keeping other teams shipping and running; embedded_systems: compiled against hardware or a kernel; forward_deployed: production code inside a NAMED customer's environment; frontend: owns UI and does not also own the server; full_stack: explicitly owns BOTH, exclusive; infrastructure_platform: consumers are other engineers or services, largest bucket, **not a catch-all**; ml_engineering: changes weights or builds what produces and serves them, success is a model metric; mobile: an app binary; qa_testing: test coverage or test infrastructure itself; quantitative: correctness measured in money and microseconds; robotics_autonomy: senses/plans/actuates in the physical world; security: the subject matter is security AND the deliverable is code); **(3)** the **ten tie-breakers with worked examples** — infrastructure_platform vs devops_sre "build it or keep it running"; robotics_autonomy vs embedded_systems "the problem you solve vs the layer you run at"; backend vs infrastructure_platform "who consumes your code" ("Software Engineer, Payments API" → backend, "SDE, AWS OpenSearch" → infrastructure_platform); data_engineering vs backend "pipeline or request-serving"; ⚠ **qa_testing vs embedded_systems — "validation" is a trap: "Silicon Validation Software Engineer — GPU IP" → embedded_systems**; full_stack only on an explicit signal, never defaulted; ai_engineering vs ml_engineering "do you change the weights?"; **NEVER route on an AI mention**; quantitative keys on the DOMAIN not the word "quant"; tags precedence; **(4)** eight worked examples; **(5)** quick canonical mappings.

Line 9 (the prose contract): after `exactly one \`level\``, insert `, an ordered \`subcategories\` array (0-2, software_engineering only)`. 3.   **THE COMPACT §1b (3,006 chars, 30 lines, measured):** a preamble ("Reached ONLY after section 1 has already returned `software_engineering` … **the arrow never runs backwards** … emitting one on any other category is a measured error"), a paragraph stating **"`[]` is a legitimate and common answer"**, the 15-row `| slug | the role's core deliverable |` table, a pointer line — **"The full per-slug rubric, tie-breakers and worked examples live in `SUBCATEGORIES.md`"** — and ⚠ **exactly THREE inline rails: `full_stack` is exclusive; never route on an AI mention; ai_engineering vs ml_engineering — do you change the weights?** **WHY THESE THREE AND NOT THE OTHER SEVEN:** `full_stack` exclusivity is the only rule whose violation **silently widens a FILTER** (a `[full_stack, backend]` row appears under both) and no downstream code can reconstruct the intent; the AI-mention trap and the weights question are the two highest-frequency confusions and cheap to state. Everything else is a precision refinement the subcategory-only lane pays for and the classify lane does not.

THE ACCESSOR NAME IS `read_subcategory_rubric` (§0 ruling 11); the contract's `subcategory_rubric()` returning `''` is overruled — a silent empty rubric is the failure mode that would make the backfill label from nothing.** ⚠ **Leave `content_hash(text=None)` UNCHANGED (SKILL.md-only) so the v6 baseline stays reproducible from a git blob.** Change `taxonomy_version()` (**:89-92**) to `f"{declared_version(text)}+{combined_hash()}"` where `combined_hash()` is sha256 over `skill_text + '\x00' + rubric_text`, first 12 hex. **The recorded string keeps the `v<N>+<hash>` shape, so `_TAXONOMY_VERSION_RE` and `_version_below` are untouched.** ⚠ **VERIFIED SAFE: `content_hash()` has zero callers outside `taxonomy.py`, and `taxonomy_version()`'s four callers are dashboard.py:135 (wrapped in `except OSError`), metrics.py:123-125 (same), cli.py:148 (`taxonomy-version`) and cli.py:472 (the record path) — `FileNotFoundError` is an `OSError` subclass, so the two guarded callers degrade exactly as they already do for a missing SKILL.md.** `cli health` asserts both files exist and prints `taxonomy_version()`, **so a missing rubric is caught by the deploy sanity check rather than at the first classify.**
  ⚠ **WHEN THE RUBRIC IS LOADED (the whole point of D3):** SKILL.md (with compact §1b) → every classify call, every judge call, both sandboxed agent specs. **SUBCATEGORIES.md → ONLY `ENR-PB-1`'s `build_subcategory_prompt`, `EV-7`'s pre-labeller, and a human reading it. Never the classify prompt, never the judge prompt, never classifier.md or judge.md.**

**`ENR-CLS-1` ⚠ — `cli.py` classify recording: the absent-vs-empty sentinel, validation, the tri-state-safe coercer, and the tick counters**

- *files:* `src/job_enricher/cli.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`
- *changes:* In `record_enrichment_items` (**cli.py:373-488**), immediately after `tax.validate_facets(...)` at **:440**: `raw = item.get('subcategories', _MISSING)` using a NEW module-level `_MISSING = object()` sentinel. ⚠ **This is the single most subtle line in the workstream: if the model OMITTED the field on an SWE row we must store NULL so the backfill lane picks the row up later; if it emitted an explicit `[]` we must store `'[]'` so the lane skips it forever. JSON cannot distinguish those after the fact, and `item.get('subcategories')` collapses them.** Under D3 this matters MORE, not less: the classify lane now works from the compact §1b, so "the model said nothing" is a more common and more meaningful outcome. `subcats, sub_bad, sub_warnings = tax.validate_subcategories(category, None if raw is _MISSING else raw)`. Fold `sub_bad` into the EXISTING needs_human + `nulled` path at **:442-449 verbatim** — same log shape, same counter — so a hallucinated slug routes to the E4 handling that already exists. Log each `sub_warnings` entry at INFO and count it as `subcategory_truncated`; ⚠ **a truncation or a full_stack suppression must NOT set `needs_human`.** Add `_coerce_subcategories_field(value) -> str | None`: `None` → `None`, list → `json.dumps(value)`. ⚠ **DO NOT reuse `_coerce_json_field` (cli.py:313-314) — verified, it does `json.dumps(value if value is not None else [])`, i.e. it turns None into `'[]'` and would destroy the tri-state on the very first write. Put that sentence in a comment beside it so nobody "simplifies" it back.** Coerce `subcategory_confidence` with the existing `_coerce_confidence` (**:317-328**) — **a dict here is precisely the class of bug commit `bf3be25` fixed after a dict confidence crashed the sqlite bind and silently stalled classify.** Pass `subcategories`, `subcategory_confidence` and `subcategory_source='classify'` into the dict handed to `store.record_enrichment` at **:460-476** — ⚠ **but source stays None when `subcats is None`, so a never-evaluated row is not falsely stamped with a source it never had.** Extend the returned counts dict (**:481-488**) with `subcategory_recorded`, `subcategory_missing` (absent on an SWE row — the backfill-eligible count), `subcategory_empty` and `subcategory_truncated`, and print them in `_cmd_record_enrichment`'s summary (**:491-509**).
  ⚠ **CORRECTED FROM THE DRAFT (§0 ruling 8): THIS STEP DOES BUMP THE TICK COUNTERS.** Call `store.bump_tick_counters(swe_classified=…, swe_subcat_evaluated=…, swe_subcategorized=…)` once per batch — `swe_classified` for every recorded row whose category is `software_engineering`, `swe_subcat_evaluated` for those whose subcategories value is **non-NULL (including `[]`)**, `swe_subcategorized` for those whose list is **non-empty**. **The draft refused on the grounds that `bump_tick_counters` silently drops unlisted keys (:632-634) — true, but all three ARE in `_TICK_COUNTER_COLS` via `ENR-ST-1`, and without these bumps BF-11's two breakers read 0 forever while looking healthy.**
  ⚠ **EXPLICITLY do NOT add any subcategory clause to `_needs_judge` (:514-527)** — roughly half the corpus is `software_engineering`, so a `SWE and subcategories IS NULL` clause would route half of it to the judge and blow up judge volume on a lane already at capacity. **Comment saying so, plus a standing regression test.**
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-TAX-1","ENR-ST-2","ENR-ENG-2"]`
- *tests:* SWE row with `['backend']` records `'["backend"]'` and `source='classify'`; non-SWE row carrying `['backend']` → NULL, needs_human set, `nulled_facets` incremented; ⚠ **ABSENT key on an SWE row → NULL, counted `subcategory_missing`, source NULL**; ⚠ **explicit `[]` on an SWE row → `'[]'`, counted `subcategory_empty` — asserted on the DB, not the counters (the tri-state end to end)**; a dict or int in `subcategories` never raises and nulls the field; a dict `subcategory_confidence` coerces to None; three slugs truncate to two **WITHOUT** needs_human; `['full_stack','backend']` stores `'["full_stack"]'`; a standing test that `_needs_judge` does NOT fire on an SWE row whose only defect is NULL subcategories. ⚠ **PLUS: a 3-row batch (one labelled, one `[]`, one absent) increments `swe_classified` by 3, `swe_subcat_evaluated` by 2 and `swe_subcategorized` by 1 — asserted on the ticks ROW.**
- *acceptance:* `uv run pytest tests/test_cli.py -q` green; feeding a hand-written 3-item JSON payload through `cli record-enrichment` against a scratch DB prints a summary carrying the four new counts, and the DB shows NULL, `'[]'` and `'["backend"]'` in three distinct rows. · *risk:* medium · *effort:* M

**`ENR-LAT-1` — The v6→v7 classify-prompt cost, turned into a written go/no-go number** *(was `PHASE-0`)*

- *files:* `docs/handoffs/2026-08-20-subcategory-phase0.md`
- *changes:* **NARROWED:** the seconds-per-row measurement for the subcategory-only prompt is DELETED from this step — it is `P0-5`'s. **Three workstreams measuring the same thing is how the drafts got here.** What remains is the one measurement nobody else owns and that D3 exists because of.
  **(A) CHARACTER COST** — already computed, to be **re-asserted against the committed files rather than re-derived**: v6 SKILL.md = **24,479 chars / 24,619 bytes / 157 lines**; classify scaffold = 1,729; judge scaffold = 2,246; a one-row job block with a 3,000-char description = 3,205; today's batch=1 classify prompt = **29,413 chars, of which SKILL.md is 83.2%**. Compact §1b = 3,006; other v7 SKILL.md edits ≈ 1,300; SUBCATEGORIES.md ≈ 6,906. **Therefore v7 SKILL.md ≈ 28,785 (+17.6%) and the v7 classify prompt ≈ 33,719 (+14.6%) — against ≈ 32,285 (+31.9%) and ≈ 37,219 (+26.5%) had the detailed rubric stayed inline.** ⚠ **D3 saves ≈ 3,500 chars on EVERY classify AND EVERY judge call: at ~38 classify + ~17 judge calls per tick that is ≈ 192,500 chars ≈ 48k tokens per tick.** Record the ACTUAL post-commit numbers in a table beside these predictions; **a >5% divergence means §1b grew during review and should be trimmed before the latency arm runs.**
  **(B) LATENCY, AND THE ABORT CEILING.** Build `build_classify_prompt` from the v6 SKILL.md (`git show <SKILL_MD_V6_GIT_SHA>:.claude/skills/taxonomy/SKILL.md`, the SHA `EV-2` records) and from v7 on the **SAME 20 real rows**, then run BOTH against local ollama qwen3:32b at batch=1 and record p50/p95 wall-clock. ⚠ **Run against a PAUSED worker or state plainly that the numbers are contended — the launchd tick every 10 minutes will otherwise inflate both arms unequally.** ⚠ **Call `prompts.reset_taxonomy_cache()` between arms; `_taxonomy_text` is `lru_cache(maxsize=1)` and a same-process rerun would otherwise score the v6 text twice and report a delta of zero.** Then **WRITE THE CEILING DOWN, with the arithmetic:** the lane measures **485 classifications/day against 495-552 arriving**, so state classifications-lost-per-1%-latency and pick the abort threshold from it. If the measured p50 delta exceeds the ceiling, **the pre-authorized mitigations in order** are: **(1)** trim the compact §1b table's glosses (the 15 rows are ~1,100 of its 3,006 chars); **(2)** drop the three inline rails to one (`full_stack` exclusivity, the only one whose violation silently widens a filter); **(3)** remove §1b from `build_judge_prompt` only — the judge re-derives from §1 and can be handed subcategories as a pass/fail rather than a re-derivation. ⚠ **Naming these in advance is what makes the ceiling actionable instead of a number that gets waived at 11pm.** Write both arms into the handoff **with the exact command lines**, so they are reproducible after the taxonomy changes again.
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-TAX-2","ENR-ENG-2"]` · *tests:* none — the outputs are the inputs to `ENR-ENG-2`'s `_CLASSIFY_PROMPT_MAX_CHARS` and to the phase-5 go/no-go.
- *acceptance:* The handoff doc contains: the predicted-vs-actual character table for all seven measured quantities; four latency numbers (v6 and v7, p50 and p95) **with the contention state stated**; a `_CLASSIFY_PROMPT_MAX_CHARS` value that `ENR-ENG-2`'s test then mirrors; and a written `CLASSIFY_LATENCY_CEILING_PCT` **with the classifications-per-day arithmetic behind it and the three pre-authorized mitigations in order.** · *risk:* low · *effort:* S

**`ENR-CLS-2` — `_merge_judge`: the code-enforced subcategory correction policy (four rails plus a knob)**

- *files:* `cli.py`, `enrich_config.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`, `tests/test_enrich_config.py`
- *changes:* **DECIDED POLICY:** the judge MAY correct subcategories, under four rails enforced in code exactly the way the level rails at **cli.py:1006-1017** are — because, in this codebase's own words (**judge.md:52-53**), ⚠ **"a rail the system doesn't enforce isn't a rail."** Add `JUDGE_SUBCATEGORY_CONFIDENCE_MIN = 0.8` beside `JUDGE_LEVEL_CONFIDENCE_MIN` (**verified cli.py:956**). In `_merge_judge` (**:960-1067**): **Rail A — knob kill switch.** If `judge:subcategory_corrections` is off, drop `subcategories` from `corr` before anything else and note it. **The knob exists so the lift can be MEASURED and killed in one command — which matters more under D3, because the judge is working from the compact §1b too.** **Rail B — confidence bar.** A `subcategories` correction is REJECTED when the judge's own `conf` is None or < 0.8: pop it from `corr`, append a rejected note in the same phrasing as the level rejections at **:1011**, and let the existing `rejected_corrections` mechanism count it. ⚠ **Do NOT force `needs_human` — that branch is reserved for the new_grad/entry equivalence case and forcing it here would flood the triage queue.** **Rail C — tri-state preservation.** `pick('subcategories', ...)` must default to the row's CURRENT stored value, and when that value is NULL and the judge supplied nothing, ⚠ **the merged value stays NULL. A judge pass must never turn "never evaluated" into "evaluated, none apply" — that silently drains the backfill queue and makes the coverage number that gates the 90% reveal a lie.** **Rail D — category consistency.** AFTER `category` is resolved, if the final category is not `software_engineering`, ⚠ **FORCE subcategories to `[]` — not NULL; the row WAS evaluated and the answer is "not applicable"** — regardless of what the judge said. This mirrors how a rejected level correction also drops the derived `level_includes` at :999/:1008. Then run the merged value through `tax.validate_subcategories(category, merged)` alongside the existing `tax.validate_facets` call at **:1038** and fold `bad` into the same needs_human + warning path. Return `subcategories` in the merged dict as `_coerce_subcategories_field(...)` — ⚠ **again NOT `_coerce_json_field`, which collapses NULL to `'[]'`.** `apply_judge_verdicts` (**:578-636**) then passes it to `store.record_judge`, whose facet-change detection `ENR-ST-2` extended. `enrich_config.py`: add `judge:subcategory_corrections` (bool, **default TRUE**) following `get_judge_enabled` (**:133-137**), its `KNOB_META_KEYS` entry (**:291-306**), its `config_snapshot` value (**:270-285**) so it rides /metrics, and its `set-*` subcommand in cli.py's knob table (**:1348**).
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-CLS-1","ENR-ENG-2"]`
- *tests:* a valid correction at conf 0.9 applies; the same at 0.7 is rejected, counted in `rejected_corrections`, noted, **and does NOT set needs_human**; conf None rejects; with the knob off nothing applies at any confidence; a judge whose correction moves category off `software_engineering` yields `'[]'` even when it also proposed `['backend']` (Rail D); ⚠ **a judge pass on a row whose stored subcategories is NULL leaves it NULL — assert `is None`, not `== '[]'` (the Rail C regression test)**; a hallucinated slug from the judge is nulled and flags needs_human; **every existing level-rail test still passes unchanged.** Config: default TRUE, `KNOB_META_KEYS` set-equality against the getters, `config_snapshot` carries the key.
- *acceptance:* `uv run pytest tests/test_cli.py tests/test_enrich_config.py -q` green; `cli config-snapshot` shows `judge_subcategory_corrections: true`. · *risk:* medium · *effort:* M

**`ENR-WB-1` ⚠ — `writeback.py`: ship subcategories behind a knob, with NULL preserved and no re-send amplification**

- *files:* `writeback.py`, `enrich_config.py`, `tests/test_writeback.py`, `tests/test_enrich_config.py`
- *changes:* **NARROWED:** the `subcategory-reopen` CLI command is DELETED from this step — D2 replaces a one-shot bulk re-open with `BF-12b`'s throttled drain over `subcategory_sent_at`, so `cli.py` leaves this file list entirely. `_row_to_result` (**verified writeback.py:35-66, it takes only `row`**): when the knob is on, add `"subcategories": _loads(row['subcategories'], None)` next to `"category"` at **:47** and `"subcategory_confidence": row['subcategory_confidence']`. ⚠ **The `_loads` default is `None`, NOT `[]` — JVN must distinguish "not evaluated" from "none apply", and the existing `_loads` helper (:36) already returns the default on a falsy/unparseable value.** When the knob is off, **omit both keys entirely.** Thread the flag in as a parameter from `write_back` (**:127**), **read ONCE per run, rather than reaching for the store inside the row loop.** ⚠ **WHY A KNOB — the corrected reason:** `EnrichmentResultItem` (**JVN models.py:879, `model_config` at :906**) sets `alias_generator=to_camel, populate_by_name=True` and **NOTHING else — no `extra='forbid'`** — so Pydantic's default `ignore` means an early `subcategories` field is **SILENTLY DROPPED and the row still counts as `written`. The failure is not a 422 you would notice; it is a clean-looking success that persists nothing.** The knob makes the deploy order reversible without a code push, **and the acceptance check below is therefore a round-trip READ, never a `written: N` count.** `enriched_unsent` (**store.py:542**) already selects `e.*`, so the new columns ride along with no query change — verified. `enrich_config.py`: add `writeback:send_subcategories` (bool, **default FALSE — ships dark**), plus its `KNOB_META_KEYS` entry, its `config_snapshot` entry, and its `set-*` subcommand. ⚠ **The knob may only be flipped ON after JVN's `EnrichmentResultItem` has the field (`SCHEMA-4`) AND `enrichment_writer` has `SUBCATEGORY_SLUGS` and the column write. That is now a real edge, not prose.**
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-ST-2","ENR-CLS-1","SCHEMA-4"]`
- *tests:* ⚠ **THE CERTIFIED ROUND TRIP, enricher side.**
  1. **`test_subcategory_round_trip_read_back`** — with the knob ON against a local JVN carrying `SCHEMA-4`: `write_back` one row, then **`JVNClient.get(f'/api/jobs/{source_id}/{job_id}')` and assert the returned `subcategories == ['backend']`. Assert on the READ, never on `written: N` — a silent Pydantic drop inflates exactly that count, which is the whole reason this test exists.** State that in the test's docstring.
  2. **`test_written_count_alone_is_not_evidence`** — a respx stub returning `{"written": 1}` for a request whose body carried the field, paired with a read-back returning no `subcategories`, **fails the round-trip helper. This pins the assertion shape so nobody "simplifies" test 1 into a count check.**
  3. The payload **omits both keys when the knob is off** and includes them when on; ⚠ **the three column states NULL / `'[]'` / `'["backend"]'` map to JSON `null` / `[]` / `['backend']` — asserted on the PARSED request body, and specifically that NULL is neither the string `'[]'` nor `[]`**; JVN `warnings[]` mentioning a soft-nulled subcategory are logged by the existing `_log_warnings` (**:69**). Config: default FALSE, `KNOB_META_KEYS` set-equality, `config_snapshot` carries the key.
- *acceptance:* With the knob ON against a JVN that has shipped `SCHEMA-4`: write back one row, then **GET it back from JVN and assert the subcategory array is present and equal.** With the knob OFF: assert the request body has **no `subcategories` key at all.** · *risk:* low · *effort:* S

**`ENR-CLS-3` — `cli.py`: eval prediction rows carry subcategories on BOTH artifacts**

- *files:* `cli.py`, `tests/test_cli.py`
- *changes:* ⚠ **`_eval_classify_pred` (cli.py:768-782) and `_eval_post_judge_pred` (:785-814) are hand-written key lists — a field absent from them is silently absent from every eval artifact, and every subcategory metric reads 0.0 with no error anywhere.** Under D3 this is the ONLY channel that can tell you whether the compact §1b is good enough, so it cannot be an afterthought. Add `'subcategories': item.get('subcategories')` and `'subcategory_confidence': item.get('subcategory_confidence')` to `_eval_classify_pred`'s returned dict, ⚠ **preserving `None` rather than defaulting to `[]` — the eval must distinguish "the model omitted it" from "the model said none apply", and that distinction IS a metric (the direct readout on whether the compact rubric is being seen at all).** In `_eval_post_judge_pred`, add `'subcategories': _coerce_subcategories_field(cp.get('subcategories'))` to the `base` dict handed to `_merge_judge`, and `'subcategories': (None if merged['subcategories'] is None else json.loads(merged['subcategories']))` to its return — mirroring how level_includes/tags/locations are dumped in and loaded out at **:790-792** and **:802-804**. **Kept as its own step deliberately: it is the interface the evals workstream builds its scorer on, so it needs an id other steps can depend on.**
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-CLS-1","ENR-CLS-2"]` · *tests:* a classify prediction row carries both keys; ⚠ **`None` survives as `None` through BOTH functions (not coerced to `[]`)**; a post-judge prediction row reflects a judge correction that passed the 0.8 rail, and reflects `[]` when the judge moved the category off SWE. · *acceptance:* `cli eval-run --engine ollama` on a 2-row scratch golden set produces both artifacts and `jq '.[0] | has("subcategories")'` is true on each. · *risk:* low · *effort:* S

### PR-D — Evals *(must precede any v7 gate decision)*

> ⚠ **PR-D is also two-stage.** Stage 1 (`EV-1` … `EV-16`) has no dependency on v7 and must complete — **including the `EV-2` model run and the `EV-2b` human review** — before PR-C stage 2 merges. Stage 2 (`EV-9b`, `EV-12`, `EV-13`, `EV-14`, `EV-17`) runs after.

#### Stage 1

**`EV-1` — Artifact namespacing (`--tag`), the ungated-facet WARN, the draft-gold relabel**

- *files:* `eval/report.py`, `eval/scoring.py`, `cli.py`, `tests/test_report.py`
- *changes:* **(a)** `_predictions_path(judge, engine)` (**report.py:35-41**) gains a `tag` param and emits `predictions[.<tag>][.<engine>][.judge_off].json`; thread `tag` through `run_eval` including the prejudge load at **:153** and the scorecard stem at **:170-171**. ⚠ **Without this the v7 run silently overwrites the v6 artifact — files are namespaced by engine only today.** Add `--tag` to the `eval` parser (**cli.py:1462-1477**) and the `eval-run` parser (**:1481-1497**) and pass it into `_cmd_eval` (**:1109-1121**) / `_cmd_eval_run` (which imports `_predictions_path` directly at **:825**). **(b)** Add `missing_baseline_facets(baseline, facets) -> list[str]` to `scoring.py`. ⚠ **`regression_gate` `continue`s at scoring.py:429-432 for any facet absent from the baseline, so adding a facet before regenerating the baseline yields a gate that prints PASS and checks nothing.** `run_eval` must print `eval --gate: WARN <facet> absent from baseline — NOT checked` per facet. **(c) PROVENANCE LINE, new:** on every gated run print `eval --gate: pool=<human|draft> n=<n> groups=<…> allow_draft=<bool>` **BEFORE** the PASS/REGRESSION verdict, ⚠ **so an advisory `--allow-draft` run can never be mistaken for a gate in a terminal scrollback or a pasted handoff.** **(d) RELABEL THE DRAFT HEADLINE.** `--allow-draft` scores against `label_source='draft'` labels which `golden.py`'s module docstring defines as judge-bootstrapped, i.e. **v6's own output** — ⚠ **so those numbers measure AGREEMENT WITH v6, and a genuinely better v7 scores worse.** When `gold_quality == 'draft'`, `_headline` (**:52-73**) prints `agreement vs draft gold (NOT accuracy)` on the category/level lines and `_DRAFT_BANNER` gains one line saying so. ⚠ **Do NOT change `--allow-draft`'s exit semantics (report.py:178 and :185-187) — it stays advisory by design; `EV-2b` removes the need for it.** **Do NOT add `--compare-baseline`** (the design track asked for it; `--baseline <path>` already exists at cli.py:1466).
- *dependsOn:* `[]`
- *tests:* Extend the `_setup` fixture at **test_report.py:26-44**: `--tag v6` namespaces BOTH the predictions path and the scorecard file, and **a `--tag v7` run leaves the v6 artifact byte-identical**; a gated facet missing from the baseline prints the WARN line; the provenance line appears on every gated run and reports `pool=draft` under `--allow-draft`; the draft headline says "agreement" and not "acc". ⚠ **The four existing tests (test_report.py:47-94) must pass unchanged.**
- *acceptance:* `cli eval-run --engine ollama --judge off --limit 3 --tag v6` writes `golden/scorecards/predictions.v6.ollama.judge_off.json`; re-running with `--tag v7` produces a second file and the first is byte-identical. · *risk:* low · *effort:* S

**`EV-3` — `golden.py`: the rebuild landmine, a per-facet schema, a facet-aware `load_truth`, and a working `merge_corrections`**

- *files:* `eval/golden.py`, `cli.py`, `tests/test_golden.py` (new), `golden/seed/golden.json`
- *changes:* **DEPENDENCY CHANGE:** the draft had `dependsOn: ['EV-2']`, a serialization hack. **Removed** — EV-3 touches no prompt and `_cmd_eval_run` reads `load_seed()`, not `load_truth()`, **so it cannot disturb the v6 capture. It must be able to land first because `EV-2b` depends on it and must precede `ENR-TAX-2`.**
  ⚠ **(a) LANDMINE FIX FIRST.** `build_golden_set` (**golden.py:21**, row template :29-46) rebuilds each row from a **12-key literal**, so any key outside that template is destroyed on the next `cli build-golden-set`. ⚠ **Verified against the live seed today: the key union is SIXTEEN and the rebuild destroys FOUR — `clean_description`, `department`, `hard_case`, `human_decision`.** *(The verifier's "destroys exactly TWO" is wrong; EV-3's count is right.)* Replace the append with `{**prev, **fresh}` where `fresh` is **only the six JVN-sourced fields** (job_listing_id, source_id, title, company, raw_location, description_html).
  **(b) ROW TEMPLATE** gains, per facet: `subcategories` (list[str]|None, default None), ⚠ **`gold_subcategory_provenance`** (renamed from `subcategory_label_source` per §1.3 so it cannot be confused with the write-path `subcategory_source`, whose vocabulary is disjoint), the generic pre-label trio `<facet>_prelabel{,_model,_confidence}` (facet ∈ {category, subcategory}), and `split` ('train'|'test'|None). ⚠ **There is NO holdout today: all 273 rows serve as dev and test, and the v6 SKILL.md few-shots were drawn from golden rows, so any v7 few-shot must come from `split='train'` and is then excluded from the gate pool.**
  **(c) `load_truth(human_only=True, facet=None)`.** ⚠ **The base predicate at golden.py:59 (`if r.get('category') or r.get('level')`) makes a subcategory-only correction invisible — add `or r.get('subcategories') is not None`.** With `facet='subcategory'`, admit ONLY rows where `category == 'software_engineering'` AND `subcategories is not None` AND `gold_subcategory_provenance in _GOLD_SOURCES`, where `_GOLD_SOURCES = {'human','human_audited'}` plus `'prelabel_agreed'` **only when `golden/seed/subcategory_audit.json` exists and reports `passed: true`.** **This decoupling is what lets each facet gate on its own honest denominator.**
  ⚠ **(d) THE FAKE-N TRAP.** Add `stamp_contract_rows()` setting `subcategories: []` and `gold_subcategory_provenance: 'contract'` on every non-SWE seed row (**147 rows: 273 − 126 SWE**; free, no model call). **They make `subcategory_leak_rate` measurable at zero cost but must NEVER be admitted by `load_truth(facet='subcategory')` or the pool clears min_n=50 with zero information.**
  **(e) `merge_corrections`** (**:71-110**): the hardcoded facet tuple at **:99** becomes `('category','level','tags','subcategories')`. After the loop, if `row['category'] != 'software_engineering'`, force `row['subcategories'] = []` and `gold_subcategory_provenance = 'contract'` **in the same write**. The existing `if facet in c` guard already gives the tolerant parse (**an absent key never overwrites an existing label**) — assert it. **NO-APPEND is decided in `EV-16` and its rationale goes in this docstring; unmatched corrections are parked, not discarded.**
- *dependsOn:* `[]`
- *tests:* NEW `tests/test_golden.py`: ⚠ **a `build_golden_set` round-trip preserves `clean_description`, `department`, `hard_case`, `human_decision`, `subcategories` and `split` — the landmine test, on all four destroyed keys**; `load_truth(facet='subcategory')` ignores the category `label_source` and **EXCLUDES contract rows**; ⚠ **an audit file with `passed: false` (or absent) drops every `prelabel_agreed` row**; `merge_corrections` folds `subcategories`, and a correction moving category off `software_engineering` nulls them; a correction lacking a facet key leaves that facet's existing gold label intact.
- *acceptance:* On a fixture seed a `build_golden_set` round-trip loses zero keys; ⚠ **on the real seed `load_truth(facet='subcategory')` returns 0 (NOT 147) before any bootstrap, and `load_truth()` still returns exactly 21** (verified today: 273 rows, 252 draft / 21 human). · *risk:* medium · *effort:* M

**`EV-4` — `subcategory_rules.py`: the shared title+tag rule labeler (weak eval baseline AND the backfill's free layers)**

- *files:* `src/job_enricher/subcategory_rules.py` (new — verified absent), `tests/test_subcategory_rules.py`
- *changes:* ⚠ **A PRODUCTION module, deliberately not `eval/weak_subcategory.py`: the backfill's free layers import the same code, and two implementations would drift until "the baseline the model must beat" stopped describing what actually ran. THIS STEP OWNS THE FILE — deleted `BF-3` must import it, not reimplement it.** API: `label_from_title(title) -> list[str]` (seniority-strip, first-match-wins priority buckets over all 15 slugs from `taxonomy.SUBCATEGORIES`, ≤2 results in priority order); `label_from_tags(tags) -> list[str]` (synonym-collapse — `c-plus-plus`→`c++`, `csharp`→`c-sharp`, `cloud|cloud-computing|cloud-infrastructure|cloud-native`→`cloud`, `embedded|embedded-systems`→`embedded` — then a tag→slug map, ⚠ **returning `[]` when it maps to zero or ≥2 buckets: no guessing**); `rule_label(title, tags) -> tuple[list[str], str]` returning (slugs, source ∈ {'title','tag',''}). **Hard rules, in the docstring and enforced by tests:** never reads descriptions; ⚠ **never routes on the `ai` tag or on AI mentions (the decision record measured 2,462 SWE rows — 22% of the corpus — carrying an ai/ml-family tag while being ordinary backend/full-stack work, and 11.5% of plain SWE descriptions mentioning LLMs)**; ⚠ **"validation" in a hardware posting means silicon bring-up → `embedded_systems`, never `qa_testing`**; `full_stack` suppresses `frontend`/`backend`. ⚠ **`forward_deployed` and `quantitative` have ZERO tag coverage in the corpus — assert that explicitly rather than letting a reader assume the tag layer covers them.** ⚠ **NEVER enumerate the 15 slugs here: iterate `taxonomy.SUBCATEGORIES` so the shared contract's canonical list stays the single arbiter.**
- *dependsOn:* `["ENR-TAX-1"]`
- *tests:* NEW `tests/test_subcategory_rules.py`: synonym collapse maps each listed variant; a title naming one specialty yields that slug; a multi-hit title yields ≤2 in priority order; a no-specialty title yields `[]`; ⚠ **`'Silicon Validation Software Engineer — GPU IP'` → `['embedded_systems']`**; ⚠ **a bare `ai` tag yields `[]`**; `full_stack` never co-occurs with `frontend`/`backend`; ⚠ **every slug in `taxonomy.SUBCATEGORIES` (all 15) has at least one title pattern**; `forward_deployed`/`quantitative` have no tag mapping.
- *acceptance:* Green, **and a one-liner over the 126 gold-SWE golden titles prints the typed/untyped split — that number is `PARAM_REGEX_COVERAGE` and sizes `EV-9b`'s tier A.** · *risk:* low · *effort:* M
